How much should Elise take on?

The help you’d like
Full planning
From your first idea to the final dance, I’ll guide you through all of it, so you can enjoy every step.
Partial planning
Already on your way and need a little extra support? I’ll step in where needed and help bring it all together.
Wedding day coordination
You’ve planned it all, now it’s time to hand it over. I’ll make sure everything runs smoothly, so you can be fully present.

Tell me everything
Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

Usually found
with 17 tabs open
At an event
probably checking the timeline
Off duty
pilates, dinner with friends and planning my next trip
Love language
having already thought of it

a plan B

570+ guests
25 May 2026

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen
Tell me everything
How many people are coming?

100

125
Cinco Cero
570+
Pro League Awards ’26

Fewer than Cinco Cero’s 125, a long table or a few round ones.

Tell me everything
…there’s nothing left to do but celebrate.

How much should Elise take on?

The help you’d like
Full planning
From your first idea to the final dance, I’ll guide you through all of it, so you can enjoy every step.
Partial planning
Already on your way and need a little extra support? I’ll step in where needed and help bring it all together.
Wedding day coordination
You’ve planned it all, now it’s time to hand it over. I’ll make sure everything runs smoothly, so you can be fully present.

Tell me everything
Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

with 17 tabs open

25 May 2026

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen
Tell me everything
How many people are coming?

Cinco Cero

Pro League Awards ’26

Fewer than Cinco Cero’s 125, a long table or a few round ones.

How much should Elise take on?

The help you’d like
Full planning
From your first idea to the final dance, I’ll guide you through all of it, so you can enjoy every step.
Partial planning

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

The day itself

And a lot of little decisions.

How much should Elise take on?

The help you’d like
Full planning
From your first idea to the final dance, I’ll guide you through all of it, so you can enjoy every step.
Partial planning
Already on your way and need a little extra support? I’ll step in where needed and help bring it all together.
Wedding day coordination
You’ve planned it all, now it’s time to hand it over. I’ll make sure everything runs smoothly, so you can be fully present.

You hold all 17. Elise takes the day itself. Usually found with 17 tabs open.

Tap a tab to hand it over as well.

Tell me everything
Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

Usually found
with 17 tabs open
At an event
probably checking the timeline
Off duty
pilates, dinner with friends and planning my next trip
Love language
having already thought of it
Non negotiable
a plan B

And then, the night came alive.

So when the doors open…
Her nights so far

570+ guests
25 May 2026

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen
Tell me everything
How many people are coming?

100

125
Cinco Cero
570+
Pro League Awards ’26

Fewer than Cinco Cero’s 125, a long table or a few round ones.

Tell me everything
…there’s nothing left to do but celebrate.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

How much should Elise take on?

The help you’d like
Full planning

Tell me everything
Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Plan my celebration

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Hire Elise for your event

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Hire Elise for your event

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Hire Elise for your event

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

Type A, so you don’t have to be.

I’m Elise, founder, planner and the person behind Honey it’s Handled.

I’m the person who has the timeline open before breakfast, knows where the florist is, notices the missing place card and has a backup plan for the backup plan.

Before starting Honey it’s Handled, I spent 5 years managing events and bringing together people, suppliers, timings, budgets and approximately a thousand moving parts.

“HONEY” BECAUSE WE’RE THE REASSURING VOICE IN THE CHAOS. “IT’S HANDLED” BECAUSE ONCE YOU CALL US, IT GENUINELY IS.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.

Tell me everything

Inside the figures, the Cinco Cero aftermovie from her page

Cinco Cero

A 50th birthday · 28 August 2026

125 guests, Mexican food and one night to celebrate 50.

The brief wasn’t simply to organise a birthday party. It was to create an evening people wouldn’t want to leave.

“My 50th birthday party has become an incredible memory, thanks to the collaboration with Honey it’s Handled!”

Jan Geysen

Fewer than Cinco Cero’s 125, a long table or a few round ones.

Pro League Awards ’26

A corporate award show · 25 May 2026 · the Handelsbeurs, Antwerp

570+ guests. One iconic venue.

On May 25th, 2026, the Handelsbeurs in Antwerp set the stage for the Pro League Awards, bringing together over 570 guests to celebrate the very best of Belgian football.

Behind the scenes, it was all about planning, precision and teamwork. A production where timing was everything, teamwork made the difference and every detail mattered.

Photos by Kaat DM. Words from her Pro League Awards page.
